{{Yearsinfilm}}
'''2024 in film''' is an overview of events, including the highest-grossing films, award ceremonies, critics' lists of the best films of 2024, festivals, a list of country-specific lists of films released, and movie programming. [[Columbia Pictures]] and [[Metro-Goldwyn-Mayer]] (MGM) celebrated their [[Centennial|100th anniversaries]]; [[Toei Company]] celebrated its 75th anniversary; [[DreamWorks Pictures]] and [[DreamWorks Animation]] celebrated their 30th anniversaries; and the first [[Mickey Mouse]] films, including ''[[Steamboat Willie]]'' (1928), entered the [[public domain]] this year. Three of the films, ''[[Inside Out 2]]'', ''[[Deadpool & Wolverine]]'', and ''[[Moana 2]]'' made $1 billion in 2024.

Alongside new releases, multiple popular films like ''[[The Lion King]]'' (1994), ''[[Les Misérables (2012 film)|Les Misérables]]'' (2012), ''[[Alien (film)|Alien]]'' (1979), ''[[Star Wars: Episode I – The Phantom Menace]]'' (1999), ''[[The Mummy (1999 film)|The Mummy]]'' (1999 film), ''[[Whiplash (2014 film)|Whiplash]]'' (2014), ''[[The Texas Chain Saw Massacre]]'' (1974), ''[[Shrek 2]]'' (2004), ''[[Twister (1996 film)|Twister]]'' (1996), ''[[Saw (film)|Saw]]'' (2004), ''[[Coraline (film)|Coraline]]'' (2009), ''[[The Nightmare Before Christmas]]'' (1993), ''[[Hocus Pocus (1993 film)|Hocus Pocus]]'' (1993), ''[[Interstellar (film)|Interstellar]]'' (2014), and ''[[Tenet]]'' (2020) were re-released to either celebrate their anniversaries and/or fill in the gaps left by films that had their original release dates affected by the [[2023 Hollywood labor disputes]]. These disputes, including the [[2023 Writers Guild of America strike|Writers Guild of America strike]] and [[2023 SAG-AFTRA strike|SAG-AFTRA strike]], had a significant impact on the 2024 release schedule, with many films being postponed due to productions being halted mid-filming or before commencement.

===Box office records===
*''[[Inside Out 2]]'' surpassed ''[[The Lion King (2019 film)|The Lion King]]'' to become the [[List of highest-grossing animated films|highest-grossing animated film]] of all time and became the [[List of highest-grossing films|8th highest grossing film of all time]] upon its release.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.thewrap.com/deadpool-3-leads-labor-day-weekend-crosses-600-million-domestic/|title=''Deadpool 3'' Leads Labor Day Weekend, Crosses $600 Million Domestic|date=1 September 2024|first=Jeremy|last=Fuster|website=[[TheWrap]]|access-date=1 September 2024}}</ref>
**''Inside Out 2'' became the 54th film to gross $1 billion worldwide<ref>{{Cite web |last=Chlidress |first=Erik |date=1 July 2024 |title=Weekend Box Office: Inside Out 2 Outpaces Barbie to Reach $1 Billion Worldwide |url=https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/article/weekend-box-office-inside-out-2-outpaces-barbie-to-reach-1-billion-worldwide/ |access-date=9 July 2026 |website=Rotten Tomatoes}}</ref> and the 12th animated film to do so.<ref name="ootw" />
**It became the fastest animated film to gross $1 billion worldwide.<ref name="ootw">{{Cite web |last=Tartaglione |first=Nancy |date=30 June 2024 |title='Inside Out 2' Out Of This World!: Sequel Crosses $1B Global Box Office; Fastest Animated Movie Ever To Milestone |url=https://deadline.com/2024/06/inside-out-2-1-billion-global-box-office-1235997925/ |access-date=30 June 2024 |website=Deadline |language=en-US}}</ref>
**The [[Inside Out (franchise)|''Inside Out'' film series]] grossed over $2.5 billion with the release of ''Inside Out 2''.{{cn|date=January 2025}}
*''[[Deadpool & Wolverine]]'' became the 55th film to gross $1 billion worldwide.
** It had the highest-grossing opening weekend ever for an R-rated film.<ref name="dwall">{{Cite web |last=D'Alessandro |first=Anthony |date=28 July 2024 |title='Deadpool & Wolverine': All The Box Office Records Broken |url=https://deadline.com/2024/07/deadpool-wolverine-box-office-records-1236025175/ |access-date=28 July 2024 |website=Deadline |language=en-US}}</ref>
**It surpassed ''[[Joker (2019 film)|Joker]]'' (2019) as the highest-grossing R-rated film of all time.<ref name="dwall" />
**It is the eleventh MCU film to gross over $1 billion, and the first since ''[[Spider-Man: No Way Home]]'' (2021).{{cn|date=January 2025}}
**It is the second R-rated film to gross over $1 billion, and the first since ''[[Joker (2019 film)|Joker]]'' (2019).<ref>{{Cite web |last=Whitten |first=Sarah |date=11 August 2024 |title='Deadpool & Wolverine' tops $1 billion at the global box office |url=https://www.cnbc.com/2024/08/11/deadpool-wolverine-tops-1-billion-at-the-global-box-office.html |access-date=2 July 2025 |website=CNBC |language=en}}</ref>
** The [[Marvel Cinematic Universe]] became the first [[film franchise]] to gross $30<ref name="dwall" /> and $31 billion<ref>{{Cite web |last=Malhotra |first=Rahul |date=16 September 2024 |title=The MCU Hits a Marvelous Global Box Office Milestone Thanks to ‘Deadpool & Wolverine’ |url=https://collider.com/marvel-cinematic-universe-global-box-office-31-billion/ |access-date=2 July 2025 |website=Collider |language=en}}</ref> with the release of ''Deadpool & Wolverine''.
*''[[Moana 2]]'' became the 56th film<ref name="PeopleDM">{{Cite web |title=Despicable Me Breaks Record to Become First Animated Franchise to Cross $5 Billion at Box Office |url=https://people.com/despicable-me-first-animated-franchise-to-cross-usd5-billion-8678626 |access-date=2 July 2025 |website=People.com |language=en}}</ref> and the 13th animated film to gross $1 billion worldwide.{{cn|date=January 2025}}
** The ''[[Moana (franchise)|Moana]]'' film series grossed over $1.5 billion with the release of ''Moana 2''.{{cn|date=January 2025}}
* The ''[[Despicable Me]]'' film franchise became the first animated film series to gross $5 billion with the release of ''[[Despicable Me 4]]''.<ref name="PeopleDM"/>
* ''[[Wicked (2024 film)|Wicked]]'', the first installment in the [[Wicked (musical)|musical's]] [[Wicked (film franchise)|two-part adaptation]], became the highest-grossing film based on a work set in the [[Land of Oz]].{{cn|date=January 2025}}
** It became the first musical film to gross over $164 million worldwide on its opening weekend, making it the highest opening worldwide gross for a film based on a stage musical since ''[[Les Misérables (2012 film)|Les Misérables]]'' (2012).<ref>{{cite web | url=https://deadline.com/2024/11/box-office-wicked-gladiator-ii-1236184897/ | title='Wicked' & 'Gladiator II' AKA 'Glicked' Fuel $205M Combined Weekend, Best Pre-Thanksgiving Frame in 11 Years — Sunday AM Box Office Update | date=24 November 2024 }}</ref><ref>{{cite web | url=https://deadline.com/2024/11/wicked-global-box-office-opening-gladiator-2-international-box-office-1236186177/ | title='Wicked' Casts $164M+ Spell in Global Opening; 'Gladiator II' Thunders to $221M WW After 2nd Offshore Frame – International Box Office | date=24 November 2024 }}</ref>
** It surpassed ''[[Mamma Mia! (film)|Mamma Mia!]]'' (2008) as the highest-grossing Broadway musical film adaptation of all time.<ref name="Billboard">{{Cite magazine|url=https://www.billboard.com/lists/broadway-musical-films-biggest-box-office-wicked/|title='Wicked' & More All-Time Top-Grossing Film Adaptations of Broadway Musicals|date=11 December 2024|first=Paul|last=Grein|magazine=[[Billboard (magazine)|Billboard]]|access-date=12 December 2024}}</ref>
* The ''[[The Lion King (franchise)|Lion King]]'' film franchise grossed over $3 billion with the release of ''[[Mufasa: The Lion King]]''.
* The ''[[Dune (franchise)|Dune]]'' film series grossed over $1 billion with the release of ''[[Dune: Part Two]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |last1=McClintock |first1=Pamela |date=5 March 2024 |title=Box Office: 'Dune: Part Two' Makes Major Gains With Younger Adults |url=https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/movies/movie-news/box-office-dune-part-two-younger-adults-audience-1235842993/ |access-date=6 March 2024 |website=[[The Hollywood Reporter]] |archive-date=5 March 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240305191955/https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/movies/movie-news/box-office-dune-part-two-younger-adults-audience-1235842993/ |url-status=live}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |last1=Malhotra |first1=Rahul |date=25 March 2024 |title=Denis Villeneuve's 'Dune' Films Finally Pass $1 Billion at the Global Box Office |url=https://collider.com/dune-films-global-box-office-1-billion/ |access-date=16 December 2024 |website=[[Valnet#Collider|Collider]] |archive-date=10 September 2024 |archive-url= https://web.archive.org/web/20240910162057/https://collider.com/dune-films-global-box-office-1-billion/|url-status=live}}</ref>
* The [[Monsterverse|Monsterverse film franchise]] grossed over $2.5 billion with the release of ''[[Godzilla x Kong: The New Empire]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Tartaglione |first=Nancy |date=28 April 2024 |title=Skadoosh!: ''Kung Fu Panda 4'' Crosses $500M WW; ''Challengers'' Widens Offshore Net; ''The Fall Guy'' Starts Overseas Action – International Box Office |url=https://deadline.com/2024/04/kung-fu-panda-4-milestone-the-fall-guy-challengers-godzilla-x-kong-dune-roundup-punishment-global-international-box-office-1235897903/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240428164936/https://deadline.com/2024/04/kung-fu-panda-4-milestone-the-fall-guy-challengers-godzilla-x-kong-dune-roundup-punishment-global-international-box-office-1235897903/ |archive-date=28 April 2024 |access-date=28 April 2024 |website=Deadline |language=en-US}}</ref>
* The ''[[Kung Fu Panda]]'' film series grossed over $2 billion with the release of ''[[Kung Fu Panda 4]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Lang |first=Jamie |date=25 March 2024 |title='Kung Fu Panda 4' Pushes Franchise Past $2 Billion With Impressive Third Weekend |url=https://www.cartoonbrew.com/box-office-report/kung-fu-panda-4-pushes-franchise-past-2-billion-with-impressive-third-weekend-239293.html?cbtr=LATPOS |access-date=25 March 2024 |website=Cartoon Brew}}</ref>
* The ''[[Sonic the Hedgehog (film series)|Sonic the Hedgehog]]'' film series grossed over $1 billion with the release of ''[[Sonic the Hedgehog 3 (film)|Sonic the Hedgehog 3]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Tartaglione |first=Nancy |date=5 January 2025 |title='Sonic The Hedgehog' Franchise Zooms Past $1B Global Box Office |url=https://deadline.com/2025/01/sonic-the-hedgehog-franchise-one-billion-global-box-office-milestone-1236246684/ |access-date=5 January 2025 |website=[[Deadline Hollywood]] |publisher=[[Penske Media Corporation]] |language=en-US |archive-date=5 January 2025 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250105144123/https://deadline.com/2025/01/sonic-the-hedgehog-franchise-one-billion-global-box-office-milestone-1236246684/ |url-status=live }}</ref>
* [[Sony's Spider-Man Universe]] grossed over $2 billion with the release of ''[[Venom: The Last Dance]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |title=Sony's Spider-Man Universe Crosses A Huge Box Office Benchmark Thanks To Venom: The Last Dance |url=https://www.imdb.com/news/ni64943346/ |access-date=2 July 2025 |website=IMDb |language=en-US}}</ref>
* The ''[[Bad Boys (franchise)|Bad Boys]]'' film series grossed over $1 billion with the release of ''[[Bad Boys: Ride or Die]]''.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Malhotra |first=Rahul |date=16 June 2024 |title='Bad Boys: Ride or Die' Blazes Past Huge Global Box Office Milestone |url=https://collider.com/bad-boys-ride-or-die-global-box-office-214-million/ |access-date=21 June 2024 |website=Collider |language=en}}</ref>

===Studio records===
* ''[[Inside Out 2]]'' became the highest-grossing [[Pixar]] film of all time, surpassing ''[[Incredibles 2]]'' (2018).<ref>{{Cite magazine |last=Nolfi |first=Joey |date=10 July 2024 |title='Inside Out 2' passes 'Incredibles 2' as biggest Pixar box office hit |url=https://ew.com/highest-grossing-pixar-movie-inside-out-2-box-office-8676146 |access-date=13 July 2024 |magazine=Entertainment Weekly |language=en}}</ref>
** With the release of ''Inside Out 2'', Pixar became the first animation studio to produce five films that grossed $1 billion.<ref>{{Cite web |last=Stevens |first=Abigail |date=5 July 2024 |title=All 5 Pixar Movies That Made $1 Billion At The Box Office |url=https://screenrant.com/pixar-movies-billion-dollars-box-office/ |access-date=2 July 2025 |website=ScreenRant |language=en}}</ref>
* ''[[Deadpool & Wolverine]]'' became the highest-grossing R-rated [[Walt Disney Studios (division)|Disney]] film, surpassing the 34-year-old record set by ''[[Pretty Woman]]'' in 1990.<ref name="DeadlineOpenWknd">{{Cite web |last=D'Alessandro |first=Anthony |date=29 July 2024 |title='Deadpool & Wolverine' At $211M, Now The 6th Highest Opening Of All-Time At U.S. Box Office – Monday AM Update |url=https://deadline.com/2024/07/box-office-deadpool-wolverine-1236022761/ |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240730110346/https://deadline.com/2024/07/box-office-deadpool-wolverine-1236022761/ |archive-date=30 July 2024 |access-date=2 August 2024 |website=[[Deadline Hollywood]]}}</ref>

===Other records===
* ''[[Deadpool & Wolverine]]'' became the most viewed trailer of all time until being surpassed by ''[[Spider-Man: Brand New Day]]'' (2026).<ref>{{cite news |last1=McClintock |first1=Pamela |title='Deadpool & Wolverine' Most Watched Movie Trailer of All Time With 365M Views |url=https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/movies/movie-news/deadpool-wolverine-most-watched-movie-trailer-1235823882/ |access-date=16 December 2024 |work=The Hollywood Reporter |date=13 February 2024}}</ref>
* ''[[Wicked (2024 film)|Wicked]]'' became the best PG-rated first-day ticket pre-seller of the year and the number 3 best PG-rated first-day ticket pre-seller of all time, behind ''[[Frozen 2]]'' and ''[[The Lion King (2019 film)|The Lion King]]'' (both 2019).<ref>{{cite web |title=Universal Pictures' WICKED First-Day Ticket Sales Soar on Fandango - Boxoffice |url=https://www.boxofficepro.com/universal-pictures-wicked-first-day-ticket-sales-soar-on-fandango/ |website=Boxoffice Pro |access-date=13 October 2024 |date=10 October 2024}}</ref>
* ''[[Terrifier 3]]'' became the highest-grossing unrated film of all time.<ref>{{cite web |last1=Squires |first1=John |title='Terrifier 3' Just Became the Highest Grossing Unrated Movie of All Time! |url=https://bloody-disgusting.com/movie/3837331/terrifier-3-just-became-the-highest-grossing-unrated-movie-of-all-time/ |website=Bloody Disgusting! |access-date=16 December 2024 |date=28 October 2024}}</ref>
* 2024 is the first year since [[2019 in film|2019]] in which the top ten highest-grossing films worldwide were entirely Hollywood productions.<ref name="BOM2024"/>
